Gotchas found in session 28:
- **The user came and went again:** at the machine at the start (HIDIdleTime 0.3 s; "Run them now", "Not now" for their parts), then away; the screen locked at ~1200 s idle in the middle of the runs. A background wait for the unlock and an AskUserQuestion brought them back ("Unlocked, run them"); then the batch ran inside the next 20 minutes.
- **A sleeping thread wakes up late:** `mach_wait_until` in a command-line tool woke up to ~0.8 ms late (default QoS), which wrecked the first timing runs of `gpustart` (every "lead" was really ~0); sleep to ~1.5 ms before the deadline and spin, and run the thread at `QOS_CLASS_USER_INTERACTIVE`. The game's limiter has the same kind of overshoot (above).
- **`ioreg`'s lock key can read "unlocked" for one sample while locked:** a background `until` loop on it exited once while the screen stayed locked; require several samples in a row.
- **An empty command buffer never reaches the GPU** (it "completes" in 0.3 µs): a wake-up needs an encoder with work (a blit fill of 4 bytes is enough).
- **A process's first blit costs 0.4–1.2 ms on the CPU**: do one at startup if it would otherwise land in a frame.
- **The renderer's per-frame times without input:** `cl_latencyStats 2` prints every frame's commit, GPU-done and present times (tag 0), enough for commit-relative numbers (`build.noindex/s28/fr.py`); pump-relative numbers need input.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (three times this session): subshells or absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 27:
- **The renderer's warnings are invisible without `developer 1`** (`PRINT_WARNING` → `Com_DPrintf` in `CL_RefPrintf`): the pipeline cache's failed saves went unseen from session 19 to 27. Add `+set developer 1` when checking a renderer path (not for timing: the bench then makes ~257 draws a frame instead of 56.6).
- **`metal` and `metalref` delete each other's pipeline cache** (different hashes; `MB_OpenPipelineCache` removes other `pipelines-*` files), so alternating them starts every run from an empty cache unless each gets its own: `build.noindex/s27/ab.sh` swaps them.
- **The system Metal shader cache is one shared store** (`$(getconf DARWIN_USER_CACHE_DIR)com.apple.metal`, 2.8 GB, not per application): a cold compile can't be reproduced in the game without touching it (never). Variants it has never seen, in `archtest` (e.g. texture unit 1 alone with unusual blends), show the cold cost.
- **A bench run takes ~13.5 s** (an ABBA ×4 of 16 runs: ~3.5 minutes), and the game doesn't reset HIDIdleTime: a reading that drops between runs is a person.
- **The user came and went:** HIDIdleTime 500 s at the start, input at 14:47 and 14:54, then 85 s in the middle of a run; asked before the bench, "Run them now", "Not now" for their parts.
- **`build.noindex/s25/ab.sh` restarts its numbering per call**: a second call overwrote the first's frame logs. `s27/ab.sh` continues from the files in its `ab/`.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (once this session).

Gotchas found in session 26:
- **The screen locks itself ~20 minutes after the last input** (`CGSSessionScreenIsLocked` true at HIDIdleTime ~1350 s; it was unlocked at ~1060 s): a long run plan stalls. Session 26's drivers stop on a locked screen; the user unlocked it once. Keep the runs inside 20 minutes of the user's last input, or ask.
- **This Mac's displays share one Space** (`defaults read com.apple.spaces spans-displays` = 1, "Displays have separate Spaces" off): a fullscreen Space blanks the other display, so whatever was drawing there stops. Exclusive fullscreen and the borderless window leave it visible.
- **A window over another application hides it from its own drawing:** Chromium (the Claude app) stops rendering when occluded, so covering the 4K removes its frames; a simple layer animation doesn't reproduce what its GPU-rendered frames do to the WindowServer (`d2gpu` does).
- **The Claude app redraws the 4K at 60/s only while a session works** (a tool call running): a background batch watched by a foreground wait keeps the "none" condition; an idle session may not.
- **The user came back mid-session** (HIDIdleTime 0.0 at the last bench): the drivers' idle check stopped it; asked, "Run it now".
- **zsh doesn't split `$spec`** (session 8's gotcha, again): `${=spec}` in a `for spec in "a b c" …` loop.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (twice again): subshells or absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 25:
- **The user was at the machine again** (HIDIdleTime 0.01–0.3 s at the start) and did 6f's half at once; then left for the checks.
- **Exclusive fullscreen with a mode switch keeps the displays captured** (SDL's design, kept): the application switcher never runs, though the game gets the keys. **AppKit doesn't deliver Cmd+Tab's key-down to the first responder** (the content view's `keyDown:` never ran): catch such keys in the pump, as the input layer reads every key.
- **`in_keyboardDebug 1`** prints every key the pump gives the game: the quick way to see whether a key reaches it at all.
- **The probe's two `in_mouseRaw` settings aren't on one clock** (the HID timestamp for 0, the GCMouse handler's time for 1): compare the paths with `gcmpoll` instead. And **GCMouse's newest-call age at the pump depends on the phase between the mouse's polling and the 4 ms frame**, which drifts slowly: one 20 s run read 0.22 ms p50, the next 0.65.
- **An idle run loop makes the movement events look ~0.5 ms later than a polling game sees them** (the main thread's wake-up and dispatch): measure input paths with the game's polling loop, not `[NSApp run]`.
- **An app's first movement events coalesce until it is active:** `gcmpoll`'s first stroke carried ~2200 counts from before its recording started; re-zero positions per stroke (and skip the first).
- **Say "the bar at the top of the screen" for the menu bar** (the user asked whether it meant the Dock).
- **A hidden fullscreen Space skips frames** (session 18's gotcha): the user switched away during the feel test's `metal` run, 13% of its frames skipped.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (four times this session, also inside a `for` loop): subshells or absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 24:
- **The user was at the machine at the start again** (HIDIdleTime 0.09–3.3 s over 12 s although the brief said they may be away): asked, "Go ahead" for the runs that take the cursor; then away (375–1180 s idle during the runs).
- **macOS's `/bin/bash` is 3.2:** no associative arrays (`declare -A` fails). Port scripts and drivers stay 3.2-compatible (`ab.sh` counts with `eval`).
- **A fresh install has no pak links:** `omohaaded` started directly from a new `metal-install` found 0 pk3 files ("Couldn't load default.cfg", exit 3). `run.sh` (any run or bench of the flavor) makes the links; session 24 ran its loop by hand first.
- **A build or install tree from before 6g still holds SDL:** its bundle's `Contents/MacOS/libSDL2-2.0.0.dylib` (copied after the link; `cmake --install` never deletes), so session 24 rebuilt `metal` and `metalab` from empty trees. `metalref` of a pre-6g commit and `kbsdl` keep theirs, as they should.
- **`kbsdl` moved to another source tree** (`kbsdl-src`): CMake refuses a build tree configured from another source, so `kbreplay.sh`'s `build_flavor` wipes such a tree first (as `build.sh` does).
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (twice again): absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 23:
- **The user may be at the machine although they said they'd be away:** HIDIdleTime was 0.03 s at the start, with input every second for ~10 s. Asked (AskUserQuestion), they said "Go ahead". Ask, don't assume.
- **The synthetic mouse resets HIDIdleTime:** at the next run's start it reads ~9 s (the previous run's last posted event plus the game's 4 s tail and quit), so a check between runs needs a threshold below that (`sit.sh` stops under 2 s).
- **The Claude app's window fills the second display (the 4K)** and redraws while a session works: that display swapped 60/s during most traced runs. Other apps on screen during the sitting: RustDesk (a remote desktop app, a window on the main display: input may also come through it) and Plexamp.
- **display_trace's "showing that very frame" can miss when the times match** (1 of 1452 in SDL's exclusive traced run, while 1452 of 1455 present times sat on a swap within 2 µs): read the time match first.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (again): absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 22:
- **Don't edit a `port/` script while it runs:** bash reads a script as it goes, so `port/kbreplay.sh build` ended with "syntax error near unexpected token" after the script was edited under it (the build itself had finished). Edit, then run.
- **After an `NSAlert` closes, AppKit gives the keyboard to no window**, whether a button or the code closed it: SDL's game window stayed unfocused (and so uncaptured) after every dialog. The native layer makes its window key again (`MacOS_DialogEnd`).
- **A timer's `stopModalWithCode:` needs an event to wake the modal loop** (an empty application-defined event); `performClick:` on the alert's own button takes a click's path. The alert's buttons are found by title in its window's views.
- **`CGWindowListCopyWindowInfo` lists the Dock's full-screen window** (layer 20, alpha 1, 1920×1080) in front of every window outside a fullscreen Space: it is the Dock's transparent overlay, not something covering. Owners, layers and bounds need no permission (window names do).
- **The startup's "Abnormal Exit" dialog opens behind other applications' windows** when the game was started from a terminal: no application or window exists yet, so the app isn't active and the alert sits at the normal level without the keyboard (SDL and native alike; upstream's behavior, left).
- **An empty PID file counts as stale** (`Sys_WritePIDFile`: nothing read = stale): the quickest way to get the "Abnormal Exit" question; `run.sh` deletes PID files, so start the app binary directly (`port/kbreplay.sh dialog start`).
- **The build's objects are LLVM bitcode** (LTO), which records the source's path, and ThinLTO's summary hashes `static` symbols' names with it: two trees can't be compared object by object. Compare the IR (`-S -emit-llvm`, `-ffile-prefix-map`/`-fmacro-prefix-map` to one prefix) without the `; ModuleID`, `source_filename` and `^N = …` summary lines (`ppall.py`). Preprocessed output with `-P` keeps blank lines that move with removed `#if` lines: ignore them.
- **The compilation database has entries with an empty command** (bison's generated `yyLexer.cpp` in the game target): skip them.
- **`git rm` stages the deletion at once:** to split one work tree into several commits, `git restore --staged <paths>` (the files stay deleted) and stage the hunks per commit (`git apply --cached <hunk>.diff` for part of a file).
- **`sys_main.c:173`'s "discards qualifiers" warning is old** (every build, upstream's `Sys_PIDFileName`).
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (again this session): subshells or absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 21:
- **A CGEvent turned into an NSEvent has no window** (`[NSEvent eventWithCGEvent:]`; the documented window fields 91/92 don't help): AppKit drops its clicks and scrolls (moves still reach the key window's first responder). Field 51 gives the window number but a wrong `locationInWindow`. What works (`MsPost` in `port/kbreplay.m`): `+[NSEvent mouseEventWithType:location:…windowNumber:…]` for the window and the point, its `CGEvent` copied, the type set, and the other event's fields copied over except 41, 43, 44, 50, 51, 52, 55 and 102 (found by trying); exact points, any button number, line and pixel scrolls. Setting field 55 (the type) to another value throws inside `eventWithCGEvent:`.
- **The CGEvent mouse delta fields are integers** (`CGEventSetDoubleValueField(kCGMouseEventDeltaX, 0.4)` gives 0): posted movement has whole deltas only; real events get their fractions from the WindowServer's own record.
- **A left click posted into an inactive app activates it and isn't delivered** (the window's first click); right and other clicks are. Test tools that post left clicks must be active (the game is).
- **The bench, the fixed views and the replays capture the cursor in a window** (a timedemo or a map is "in game"): SDL always did, the native flavor does since 6d. The capture is the run's slowest frame (4–7 ms, the first).
- **GCMouse's `lastEventTimestamp` is wall-clock time** (Unix seconds), not the uptime clock; **the user's HyperX keyboard is also a GCMouse** (a mouse HID interface): don't assume one GCMouse per mouse.
- **`+[NSCursor invisibleCursor]` isn't AppKit's** (SDL adds it in a category): the native layer makes its own transparent 16×16 cursor.
- **An inner `EOF` line ends an outer `<<'EOF'` heredoc in the Bash tool**: a Python heredoc that writes a shell heredoc needs another delimiter (`PYEOF`), or write the script to a file.

Gotchas found in session 20:
- **MOHAA's console key opens nothing without `ui_console 1`** (default 0): `K_CONSOLE` reaches `CL_KeyEvent`, which calls `UI_ToggleConsole` only with `ui_console`, else the DM console in a multiplayer game. A test that needs the console sets it.
- **`g_gametype 0 +devmap` holds the loading screen** (a `KEYCATCH_UI` catcher) until a key or `finishloadingscreen` (sessions 1–3's gotcha): the first replayed keys dismissed it until the replay cfg ran `finishloadingscreen`.
- **SDL's key repeat is its own key state**, and every `flagsChanged` re-sends all eight modifier sides (a held one as a repeat key-down): replays must hold modifiers the way a keyboard does (device bits in `modifierFlags`), or SDL reports both sides down (`IsModifierKeyPressed`'s fallback).
- **In-process posted key events behave like real ones** (`[NSApp postEvent:atStart:]`: the pump, `interpretKeyEvents:` with dead keys, the menu's ⌘ equivalents). `LMGetKbdType()` in the game process is 3 until a real key event arrives (40 in a fresh tool): both layers read the same value.
- **`K_CONSOLE` prints as `0xn3`** in `Key_KeynumToString` (upstream, cosmetic).
- **Never replay Ctrl+V, Shift+Insert or ↑/↓ in a console line that enter then runs:** paste and history could run the user's clipboard or an old command. The UI console has no paste (only the notepad's `UIMultiLineEdit` does), but keep replayed lines to harmless `echo`s.

Gotchas found in session 19:
- **`initWithContentRect:styleMask:backing:defer:` without `screen:` puts the window on the main display** whatever the rect says: pass the screen and a rect relative to it (SDL does).
- **This Mac keeps the menu bar in fullscreen apps** (`defaults read NSGlobalDomain AppleMenuBarVisibleInFullscreen` = 1): a fullscreen Space then sits below the menu bar and can't go direct ("display rejected") unless the window delegate's `window:willUseFullScreenPresentationOptions:` hides it.
- **`open -a <app>` from a script can't take the focus from the game** (cooperative activation since macOS 14): focus-loss paths need the user's Cmd+Tab.
- **SDL's first pump sleeps 300 ms** (`SDL_Delay(300)` in its `applicationDidFinishLaunching:`, after activating the Dock), a hack for the menu bar after a terminal launch; the native app doesn't copy it.
- **A trace run's display check without input or the probe:** `OMM_FLAVOR=<flavor> MOUSE=0 LATSTATS=0 TRACE=1 port/latency.sh metal <name> 10 <engine args>`; `latency.sh` then ends with "no whole-run numbers" (expected). Delete the `.trace` (~0.3 GB) afterwards (`fs.sh` does).
- **gamepolicyd's log lines for one run:** `/usr/bin/log show --start "<time>" --end "<time>" --predicate 'process == "gamepolicyd"' | grep -iE "gaming session|Game mode status"`; a `tail` of the last minute shows only the session's end ("paused", "inactive").
- **CMake option order:** `USE_SDL` lives in `cmake/platforms/macos.cmake`, which the top level includes before `libraries/` and the targets, so every file sees it.
- **Comparing preprocessed sources across commits:** both trees need the same layout (a `git archive` of HEAD next to a copy of the work tree), or `#pragma once` headers get included twice from the two trees and the outputs differ for nothing.

Gotchas found in session 18:
- **The user may be at the machine even when a session was started by another session:** `HIDIdleTime` (above) said 0–1 s; the user answered at once and did 3d. Check it early.
- **A play test needs a clean config:** `home.noindex/main/configs` holds whatever the last test wrote (`s_volume 0`, `com_maxfps 0`, a test name). Session 18 moved it aside (`build.noindex/s18/configs-before-3d`) and kept the user's play config (`configs-play`); benches and shots delete the folder anyway. The user's own config is never linked (CLAUDE.md).
- **Talking to the session during a play test hides the game:** a fullscreen Space in the background takes no drawables, so `r_metalPresentStats` counts those frames as skipped (28% in session 18's run). Not a renderer problem.
- **`ninja` isn't on the port's PATH:** use `"$OMM_NINJA" -C build.noindex/metal -t compdb` (session 15's inventory method).
- **`PROTOCOL_HANDLER` is never defined** (the Info.plist's `openmohaa://` comes from `PROTOCOL_HANDLER_SCHEME`, a CMake variable only): the URL code in `sdl_input.c` and `sys_main.c` is dead today, upstream too. *Removed in session 22 (§3.1 #13), except `sdl_input.c`'s blocks (6g).*
- **The "N files in pk3 files" count grows across reconnects** (73188, then 109782 after a second `FS_Startup`): upstream's counter, cosmetic, not investigated.

Gotchas found in session 17:
- **The repo's `.gitignore` has `build*`:** a vendored tree loses files named `build…` (openal-soft's `build/.empty`, fmt's `support/build.gradle` and, on this case-insensitive volume, `support/bazel/BUILD.bazel`) unless it is added with `git add -f`. Compare the staged count with the tree's.
- **`add_subdirectory` hands a vendored project the top level's directory settings:** `add_compile_options` (warnings, `-fvisibility=hidden`, `-fno-strict-aliasing`), `CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS` (the clang workaround), `CMAKE_INTERPROCEDURAL_OPTIMIZATION`, `CMAKE_SHARED_LIBRARY_PREFIX ""` and `CMAKE_DEBUG_POSTFIX -dbg`. The last two renamed openal-soft (`openal.1.dylib`) until its target got `PREFIX lib` and an empty `DEBUG_POSTFIX`. Check a vendored library's output name and install name (`otool -D`).
- **openal-soft turns some warnings into errors by default** (`-Werror=undef`, `=format-security`, `=function-effects` without `ALSOFT_WERROR`), so a new SDK can break its default build: the macOS 26 SDK's `CA_REALTIME_API` did (Phase 7 "Session 17 result").
- **SIP strips `DYLD_*` variables through `/bin/bash`**, so `DYLD_PRINT_LIBRARIES=1 port/run.sh …` prints nothing. Run the binary itself (`…/openmohaa.app/Contents/MacOS/openmohaa +set fs_homepath "$OMM_HOME" …`, after one `run.sh` made the pak links).
- **No `timeout` on this Mac** (no coreutils): test cfgs end with `quit`, or wrap the command in `perl -e 'alarm shift; exec @ARGV' <s> …` as `latency.sh` does.
- **`port/glcheck.sh`'s Python sits in single quotes:** an apostrophe in a comment there ends the quote ("unexpected EOF").
- **`connect localhost` is the in-process loopback** ("localhost resolved to loopback"), so a client never reaches a separate `omohaaded` that way: "Connect to server timed out" every second (190k log lines). Use `connect 127.0.0.1`. A server on `net_ip 127.0.0.1` stays off the LAN; the client then takes UDP 12204 (12203 is taken).
- **Archiving sessions can remove their shared notes folders** (`~/Library/Application Support/Claude/side-session-notes/<id>`): after the user had sessions 1–16 archived, 10 of their folders were gone. The repo is the lasting state; session 17 copied the port notes that were left to `build.noindex/notes-backup/`. The app refuses to archive a session with live work: Remote Control on (turn it off first) or a background shell still running.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call still moves the session's working directory** (twice this session): use subshells or absolute paths.

Gotchas found in session 16:
- **Nothing may be added inside the installed `openmohaa.app`:** the install signs and checks it. A file added afterwards fails `codesign --verify`, and a link out of the bundle (the old `run.sh`'s `Contents/MacOS/libopenal.1.dylib`) makes the next install fail ("invalid destination for symbolic link in bundle"). Delete it and install again. Session 16 removed the old link from the `metal`, `metalab` and `metalref` installs; the `gl` reference keeps its own (unsigned bundle).
- **`cmake --install` never deletes files:** what an old rule installed stays in the prefix until it is removed by hand (session 16 removed recastnavigation's `include/` and `lib*.a` and the root `openmohaa.app` from the `metal`, `metalab` and `metalref` installs; the `gl` reference still has them, from its frozen CMake).
- **CMake names a `MACOSX_BUNDLE` after its executable** (`OUTPUT_NAME`): the .app and the binary can't have different names without a rename after the build.
- **`install(CODE)` added in `finish_macos_app` (`post_configure`) runs after the bundle's install rule** (rules run in the order they are added; the top level's `client.cmake` comes first).
- **The linker's ad-hoc signature uses 4 KB pages, `codesign`'s 16 KB** (`hashes=816` → `204`): expected.

Gotchas found in session 15:
- **What the build really uses:** `ninja -C build.noindex/metal -t compdb` (compiled sources) plus `-t deps` (every included header of every built object). `git ls-files` minus those = the files no target uses. The deps cover the Release tree's objects only: when deleting headers, also check a Debug build (a fresh `-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Debug` tree in `build.noindex/`, deleted afterwards).
- **26 objects rebuild after every commit:** `common.c` and `cl_console.c` depend on the git revision (`add_git_dependency`), and the sys files rebuild with them. A changed flag would rebuild everything.
- **A dedicated server sends GameSpy heartbeats by default** (`sv_gamespy 1`), ~~and every start (client or server) asks GitHub for releases~~ (it never did, and is compiled out since session 16): a local test server gets `+set sv_gamespy 0` (`+set com_updatecheck_enabled 0` is no longer needed; harmless). Also `+set g_gametype 1 +set sv_maxclients 2`, and a cfg with `map …`, `wait N`, `quit` (`home.noindex/main/sv_7a.cfg`).
- **The fork runs GitHub Actions on every push** (`gh run list -R pstngh/openmohaa-metal`). With the workflows deleted, nothing runs now.
- **`pak7.pk3` in the game folder is retail** (the 2002 patch pak, 89 files); `run.sh`'s `[Pp]ak[0-9]*.pk3` glob links it and skips the user's `zzz_*`/`1v1-maps` paks, as intended.
- **zsh globs in `grep --include=*.c` fail with "no matches found":** quote them (`--include='*.c'`).

Gotchas found in session 14:
- **Streamed sounds are never checked at registration** (`S_LoadSound` returns qtrue at once for them) and cgame calls `S_RegisterSound` on every trigger: per-sfx state that must survive between triggers can't be reset in `S_RegisterSound` (5c resets it in `S_BeginRegistration`).
- **Every pk3 file lookup misses the directory search paths first** (`home.noindex/main`, then the install's `main`), so anything that costs per directory miss costs per file read.
- **A `cd` in a Bash call moves the session's working directory** in this app: use subshells or absolute paths.
- **In MOHAA the 2D draws flush the render commands** (`Draw_StretchPic` and friends call `R_IssuePendingRenderCommands`), so the 3D backend runs inside cgame's draw (`CL_CGameRendering`) and `RE_EndFrame` only presents (~5–15 µs). To time the backend, time `R_IssueRenderCommands`, not `RE_EndFrame`.
- **The timedemo frame boundary is inside `CL_SetCGameTime`, before the demo messages are read:** frame N's work includes parsing frame N's snapshot (and the sounds it starts) plus the next `Com_Frame`'s `IN_Frame`/event loop.
- **A `Com_Printf` inside a frame costs time too** (console + stdout): a temporary per-frame print adds to the next frame's work. Print only the slow frames, or buffer.
- **BSD `patch` leaves `.orig` files** when a hunk applies with an offset: `build.sh gl` passes `--no-backup-if-mismatch`.
- **`ld: warning: reducing alignment of section __DATA,__common`** is old (in session 12–13 build logs too), not from the new `clc` array.

Gotchas found in session 13:
- **The bench's first run after a build can be an outlier** (metalref 0.289 vs 0.24–0.25 after it): it showed up once in 30 runs. Look at the run list, not only the mean.
- **A cache can be faster bounded than unbounded:** the collapsed index lists ran faster with a 2 MB pool and flushes (88% reuse) than with an unbounded 32 MB one (95% reuse). Size caches by measuring, not by hit rate.
- **`sample` prints C++ frames with their parameter list** (`SCR_SimpleUpdateScreen()`), so `port/sample_by_library.py`'s subtree argument missed C++ functions; fixed in session 13 (it compares the bare name now). The frame-work total of a profile is `CL_Frame` − `CL_DemoCompleted` + `IN_Frame` samples.

Gotchas found in session 12:
- **`world_t` already has `staticModels`** (the instance array): the static-buffer offset is `staticModelVerts`.
- **Static models are drawn mostly by the generic iterator** (one stage, `rgbGen vertex`, `alphaGen vertex`), not the vertex-lit one (~1% of their surfaces). Count before assuming an iterator.
- **The skinab build step can fail on a stale anchor while the bench still runs the old metalab install:** check `skinab.sh`'s last line says "installed" before reading the SKINAB line.
- **The Bash tool refuses `sleep N; cmd` chains:** wait with a background `until …; do sleep 5; done` (run_in_background) or the Monitor tool.

Gotchas found in session 11:
- **`pgrep -f '<pattern>'` in a wait loop matches the loop's own shell** (its command line contains the pattern), so the loop never ends. Wait on a PID, a log line, or `pgrep -x openmohaa` instead. The stuck waiters from earlier sessions (`until ! pgrep -f "shot.sh regress"…`, `…"prof/pairs.sh"…`) ran until session 17, when the user asked to clean up the old sessions: two were left, both in session 10, and they kept the app from archiving it ("live work"); session 17 stopped them (`ps -Aww` shows the whole command line; plain `ps -A` cuts it).
- **Metal's "safe" math mode still contracts within an expression** (front-end `fmuladd`, like clang's `-ffp-contract=on`). So `(a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y) + a.z*b.z` compiles to the same fma chain as the explicit `fma()`s. To change the rounding, change the association. MSL `uint3` in a struct is 16-byte aligned; the A/B capture struct uses `uint[3]`.
- **Vertex functions that write a device buffer** ("writable resources in non-void vertex function") are a warning, an error under the shader build's `-Werror`: `skinab.sh` drops `-Werror` in its scratch copy only.
- **`skinSurface_t` already exists** in `tr_local.h` (Q3 skins); the GPU-skinning tables are `skinCopySurface_t`/`skinCopyBoneMap_t`.
- **`TIKI_Skel_Bones` is reset per `RE_RenderScene`** (`TIKI_Reset_Caches`), so the same pointer holds other bones in the next scene: the backend's bone copies are keyed by `backEnd.viewParms.frameSceneNum` (`mbSkinDraw_t.scene`).

Gotchas found in session 10:
- **m2l1 view 0 varies by 0–17 pixels from run to run** with the same binary (the unchanged build against itself: 1 and 17 pixels). Rerun before reading a small m2l1 difference as a change. dm6 view 8 (weapon-bar highlight), dm5 (snow) and dm6fx views 4–7 (firing, grenades) vary as before.
- **`-fno-strict-aliasing` is on for the whole build** (upstream's flags): any store through a `byte`/`float` pointer forces later loads of anything else to be redone. Copy loop-invariant data (lights, bones) to locals before hot loops; that alone is exact.
- **Exactness of restructured float loops:** clang decides FMA fusion per expression in the front end (`-ffp-contract=on`), so a loop rewritten with the same macros and expressions per element rounds the same, even when it vectorizes. Different expressions (e.g. splitting `a + b*c` into two statements) do not.
- **Profiling:** `sample <pid> 20` on a looping timedemo (`prof_loop.cfg` in `home.noindex/main/`: `set nextdemo "exec prof_loop.cfg"` then `demo bench_mohdm6`) gives clean call trees; leave out `CL_DemoCompleted` (the reloads) and the limiter's `NET_Sleep`. xctrace's Time Profiler (`--attach <pid>`) can't unwind through cgame.dylib (unloaded and reloaded per demo), so its shares are skewed, but its leaf addresses give per-instruction hot spots: subtract the load slide from the trace's `load-addr` and match an `objdump --disassemble-symbols=_<fn>` of the same binary. `sample` inflates the frame (0.41 → ~0.45 ms).
- Temporary counters printed from `RE_EndFrame` (`tr_cmds.c`) every N frames are a quick way to get per-frame workload numbers (vertices, lights per vertex); revert them before committing.

Gotchas found in session 9:
- **dm6 view 7 (the spawned soldier) can differ in one run:** once in session 9 a Metal run showed 574589 pixels off (a slightly moved view, game side); the next run matched GL1 again (1 pixel). GL1 runs agree bit for bit. If it happens, rerun the Metal side (`port/shot.sh fixed metal dm6 shot_dm6.cfg`, then `port/shot.sh views dm6`).
- **`port/build.sh` wipes a build tree that was configured from another source tree** (the old `gl` tree pointed at the repo root). Its default flavor is now `metal`.
- **`build.noindex/metalref-install`** was a scratch build of the pre-deletion GL1 + Metal (dylib) for the static-link A/B; deleted at the end of session 9. To rebuild it: configure `build.noindex/gl-src` with `-DBUILD_RENDERER_METAL=ON` (see §2 Phase 4).
- **`.github/workflows` still pass the removed options** (`USE_RENDERER_DLOPEN`, `BUILD_RENDERER_GL2`) and can't compile the Metal shaders without the toolchain: other-platform CI goes in Phase 7 (deleted in 7a, session 15: all workflows).
- The `seta cl_renderer` of an old config is written back as `seta cl_renderer "metal"` (the user's archive flag stays); harmless.

Gotchas found in session 8:
- **The Bash tool runs zsh:** `for p in "a b" …; do cmd $p; done` passes "a b" as one argument; use `${=p}`.
- **`com_target_game 1/2` writes into `home.noindex/mainta/` / `maintt/`** (screenshots, configs). `shot.sh` collects `main*/screenshots` now.
- **`r_showtris`/`r_shownormals` need `developer 1`.** Cvars the renderer registers late (like a scratch cvar) need `set` in a cfg; the bare `name value` form is silently ignored when the cvar doesn't exist yet.
- **Commands after `demo` in a cfg or on the command line didn't run** (so `video` couldn't be tested).
- **The system Metal shader cache (`$(getconf DARWIN_USER_CACHE_DIR)com.apple.metal`) is shared by all apps:** don't clear it to measure cold compiles. A new pipeline variant shows the cold cost by itself (42–56 ms here).
- **The pipeline cache lives in `home.noindex/metal/`** for our runs; delete it to measure a run without it, or set `r_metalPipelineCache 0`.
- **A missing `quit` at the end of a shot cfg costs shot.sh's 120 s alarm per run.**

Gotchas found in session 7:
- **Fixed views only compare deterministic things:**
  - `tele` keeps velocity: let a spawn fall die out in noclip first (`wait 2000`);
  - spawned actors walk off, so use `spawn script_model model <tiki>`. `spawn` places the entity 40 units ahead and 40 up: spawn from 40 below the floor to stand it on the floor;
  - firing, grenades, `cg_acidtrip` and (since the compiler flag) dm5's snow differ run to run. Check a new view with two runs of one renderer (`port/shot.sh fixed metal <name>B …`) before reading a GL1 difference as a renderer one.
- **mohdm6's "spawned soldier" view (dm6 view 7) mostly shows a close wall:** the soldier is barely in it. `shot_dm6fx.cfg` view 2 is the real model view.
- **`port/shot.sh` runs the `metal` install for Metal and the `gl` reference for GL1;** `OMM_FLAVOR=<flavor>` / `OMM_GL_FLAVOR=<flavor>` run other ones (e.g. a scratch build in build.noindex/).
- **`lldb --batch` stops at a crash without running the following `-o` commands:** use `-k "bt 40"` (`--source-on-crash`).
- **Grenade timing:** a frag grenade thrown from a cfg (`useweaponclass grenade`, `+attack`, `wait 500`, `-attack`) explodes ~4.5 s after the release.
- **No PIL here:** `port/tgamontage.py out.tga <columns> <downscale> a.tga b.tga …` tiles screenshots to look at them side by side; `sips -s format png` converts TGA → PNG.

Gotchas found in session 6:
- **In zsh, `log` is a builtin:** use `/usr/bin/log show …` for the unified log (gamepolicyd: `--predicate 'process == "gamepolicyd"'`, look for "gaming session" and "Game mode status").
- **Instruments' displayed-surfaces table has `detachment-reason` and `detachment-suggestion` columns** next to `direct-to-display`. `display_trace.py` prints the reason; `PROCESS=<name>` points it at another process.
- **Window latency changes with the compositor's mode, from run to run** (2.4–13 ms p50 this session). Measure windows several times, or trace them; compare display modes in one sitting.
- **The user's display-tint daemon (`~/.local/bin/dusk tint`) is running:** it overrides display gamma tables (GL1's hardware ramp), so don't judge GL1's gamma/overbright by eye on this Mac. Never stop it; it's the user's.
- **`GAMMA:` in the startup log always says 0 overbright bits** (printed before `R_SetColorMappings`); run `gfxinfo` later in the run for the real value.
- **Fullscreen runs reach Game Mode**, which may shift scheduling. Compare modes in one sitting (Phase 5 measures Game Mode on/off).

Gotchas found in session 5:
- **`port/latency.sh` takes over the mouse:** the game window grabs it, and synthetic moves are posted for the run's length, about 30 s per run. Every run in session 5 got the keyboard focus by itself. If the user clicks elsewhere, SDL ignores the mouse and the run has no input frames. Check the screen lock first (the script does).
- **Instruments' display labels are misleading:**
  - the 240 Hz G2524H shows up as "external-0" with resolution (3840,2160), and the 60 Hz 4K as "external-1" (1920,1080): tell displays apart by their swap rate;
  - `Frame N` in surface labels starts at 1 with the first present inside the trace and wraps at 1000;
  - the presented-handler table's "Requested Time" is not on the host clock.

  `port/display_trace.py` handles all of this.
- Traces are ~0.3 GB each in `build.noindex/latency/`; `latency.sh` keeps them, so delete them after use.
- ~~Core Animation's present feedback in fullscreen with vsync off is wrong~~: fixed by going direct to display (session 6); still true for a composited fullscreen (GL1).
- macOS `sed` has no `\|` in basic regexes (use `grep -E`/awk). In zsh, `${=var}` word-splits a string variable.
- Session 4's gotchas still apply:
  - the Bash tool runs zsh (use arrays or `${=A}` for several args);
  - `wait N` with `fixedtime` continues after N/msec − 1 frames;
  - several `screenshot`s in one frame overwrite each other;
  - `r_drawworld 0` does nothing;
  - cheats need `+set thereisnomonkey 1`;
  - the weapon bar highlight runs on a real-time clock;
  - most maps crash when loaded locally (session 7: the clang bug, §3.2).
- Sessions 1–3's gotchas still apply:
  - check `CGSSessionScreenIsLocked` before measuring;
  - never pace frames on completed handlers;
  - test cfgs get a prefix (`shot_`, `lat_`);
  - `g_gametype 0 +devmap` needs `finishloadingscreen`;
  - the renderer's `vid_restart` is queued after pending cfg commands;
  - `screencapture` and ScreenCaptureKit have no permission (`CGPreflightScreenCaptureAccess` 0), but posting events does;
  - stale PID files block on the "Abnormal Exit" dialog;
  - `s_initsound 0` crashes;
  - use `sv_maxclients` ≥ 2 for scripted map loads;
  - `glconfig_t` is ABI;
  - HiDPI input on 2× displays is untested (§6 of session 4: `SDL_WINDOWEVENT_RESIZED` compares points with pixels, relative mouse deltas are in points, `SDL_WarpMouseInWindow` expects points).

Open bugs:
- **In the renderer:** none known. (The depth/stencil readback stubs are gone, session 8.)
- **Display path:** the window's latency depends on the compositor's mode (§2). In exclusive fullscreen and the borderless window, another application drawing on the other display delays our flips (the WindowServer's, p95 +~2 ms); late presents keep every frame shown (session 26), the Space avoids it on this Mac by blanking that display. (GL1's fullscreen never went direct; GL1 is gone, the reference flavor keeps it for comparisons only.)
- **Outside it:** none known. (Session 19's failed pipeline-cache save: found and fixed in session 27, Phase 5 "Session 27 result".) The local map-load crash was the Apple clang 21 `MergeCompatibleInvokes` bug; the build flag fixes it (§3.2 session 7). Drop the flag once Xcode fixes the bug.
- **Untested:**
  - ~~HiDPI input~~: the mouse replay on the 2× display (session 21: the UI's cursor in pixels; the resize check compares pixels since 6b); a real mouse on the 2× display with the native flavor is still unchecked;
  - video capture (`video`; synchronous by design, see §3.2 session 8);
  - swipes and mirrors (no retail content reaches them, §3.2 session 7); real map flare surfaces (none in retail; tested with a scratch hook);
  - ~~exclusive fullscreen at a non-native resolution (a display mode change), and fullscreen on the 4K@2× display~~: tested in session 19 (direct on every frame: 1280×720 @ 240 Hz on SDL and native, native exclusive on the 4K at 3840×2160); the Space and the borderless window on the 4K are still untested;
  - ~~exclusive fullscreen's focus loss on the native layer (needs a real Cmd+Tab, 6f)~~: the user's Cmd+Tab in session 25 (at the desktop's mode through the system's switcher; with a mode switch through `MacOS_SwitchKey`); a mode switch on a display other than the main one (`CGDisplayCapture` of that display only) is still untested;
  - GCMouse's clicks against the button events in a polled loop (session 21's measurement used an idle run loop).
